The header's **⋯** (`TaskMenu`): Edit task, Change approver (the Owner), Reopen, Cancel (reasons). the entity tabs wrap to two rows on a phone); `TeamMember.engagement`, `listCurrentCoordinators()`, `listOwnFreelancerIds()`, `listDirectoryOf(ids)` in `modules/team`; `ActivityEntry.onBehalfOfId` in `core/activity`; `listDefinitions("task", { taskTypeId })` in `core/custom-fields`; `istInstant` / `toISTTime` / `isISTTime` in `core/time`; `PlaceholderPage` takes `actions`. **Seed:** per project `task-staff|helper|away|coord|admin-<project>@maxoff.local` and a freelancer "Test Task Freelancer (<project>)" coordinated by `task-coord-…`. **Tests:** unit `modules/tasks/tests` (warnings, links, the actions per viewer, the status and route lines, the draft and the edit diff, the history sentences, the pair), `core/time` istInstant, settings threshold schema; e2e `e2e/tasks.spec.ts` (desktop and 375: each warning kind kept and recorded; the coordinator's Noted / Start / tick / Done with a link and a comment for the freelancer; the Admin's check and the Owner's approval; the Owner past a waiting Admin; changes requested, then Done again late with a reason; an Admin's task routes to them, Staff see no New task and a not-found for another's task; edit, cancel and reopen named in the history; a Settings task field filled in the dialog; installed at 375 and 430: the dialog's back and Discard order, create → the task page → back to Tasks → home, the ⋯ menu, Cancel, comment, edit, approver, add-stage, Approve, Request changes, Remove stage and Done layers, the header back, 44px targets and 16px inputs; the page and the dialog at 130% / 200%), `mobile.spec` holds the task page's skeleton (Owner, Staff) at 100/130/200%, `settings.spec` the workload field, `custom-fields.spec` the Tasks tab. **Proof:** `pnpm check` green (1064 unit in 87 files, pgTAP 2456 in 35 files, build, budget: `/approvals` 847.6 KB, `/my-day` and `/today` 774.1 KB, `/login` 526.0 KB; unbudgeted `/tasks` 774.5 KB, `/tasks/[id]` 828.0 KB); full e2e after one `db:reset` and `storage:start` at 3 workers **679 passed / 140 skipped / 0 failed, 12.6 min (started 16:47 UTC)**, the two long serial specs included. Commits: `5d8d21f` (the unit), `429d838` (the hidden coordinator's name, archived field values on an edit), then docs. **Settings left for 4C (with the reason):** **Task types** (the Owner's editor, kickoff 4 decision 15): the dialog works with the seven seeded types; the editor needs `task_type_move()` (SQL, 4A later item (b)) and its default reminders are 5.3's editor; when built, its Settings row's permission becomes `settings.manage` (today `lists.manage` shows Admins a "Coming soon" row for a list they will never edit). **Per-type task fields** in Settings → Custom fields (the column and the dialog support them; the Settings form offers company-wide task fields only). **Decisions inside the kickoff's answers (a reviewer should confirm):** (a) one dialog creates and edits; the approver changes only through the ⋯ "Change approver" (the Owner), never in the edit form; stages are typed at creation and afterwards added or removed (unticked) on the page, no rename; (b) warnings on an edit: the people added, or everyone kept when the deadline's day or the event moved, the task's own count and block excluded; (c) at most one warning per kind per person, leave on the deadline's day first; (d) saving waits for a running availability check, and a failed check never blocks (it says so); (e) back with typed changes asks "Discard?" (§14.2 f), which the app's older form dialogs do not; (f) the client label pre-selects its Admin on create only; (g) the task page is one screen with sections, no tabs, history the latest 50 entries; (h) someone who may not see a task gets the not-found screen (RLS decides, as for clients); (i) a coordinator who is not an assignee acts (Start, ticks, comment default) for the freelancer they coordinate on the task, the primary one first; (j) clients offered as labels: draft, active and paused; (k) "Task Noted" is the page's one red commit, Mark done and Approve are neutral-solid triggers, Request changes a red outline; (l) a stage tick's time is the server's clock, `done_by` the guard's; (m) a Staff co-assignee who cannot see a freelancer's coordinator reads "Asha's coordinator" instead of the pair; (n) the Tasks stand-in's copy for the Owner and Admins now says New task is here and the list is coming. Details in "4B mechanics" below. **What 4C inherits:** the task page and its actions (a list row only needs `DrillLink href=/tasks/<id>`); `NewTaskButton` and `loadTaskFormSetup`; `taskActions()` and `statusLine()` for "Needs you" and "My tasks"; the `(list)` group (replace its page, `loading.tsx` and `STAND_INS.tasksTeam` / `tasksMine`, and `loading-routes.test.ts`'s stand-in lines); read names for Staff with `listDirectoryOf(ids)`, never the whole directory (mechanics (1)). **After the unit (the 4B fixer, Opus 5.5, 2026-09-29):** `ac1a869` fixes CI run 36602212936's only red (the Settings 200% test measured the rows while React still held the streamed page in its `<div hidden>`, so every label read 0px; it now waits for `expectSettled`), `2f18d4d` makes the independent review's fixes (migration `20260929172600_phase4b_review_fixes` with pgTAP `35`: S5 the guard stamps a tick's time, S6 no label on an Inactive client, S7 an archived type kept but never chosen; S1–S4, the S8 interim, S11 and S12 in the app, with the later items under Ideas "4B later items (review)" and the PR #22 merge list in "4B mechanics (8)") and `d6d9028` adds the review's e2e (M1's back specs, S10's flows), whose first full run found what `49e39ce` fixes (a modal reopened while it still closed put its new overlay over its own sheet, blurred and untappable; Dialog, AlertDialog and Sheet now key their portals by the opening).

RLS `team.view` + the coordinator's own rows; no API writes; audited `coordinator_set` / `coordinator_changed` / `coordinator_closed`), `app.coordinator_of()`, `member_add_freelancer()`, `member_set_coordinator()` (allowed on a deactivated freelancer, so a reactivation is prepared with it), `member_invite_employee()` ("Invite as employee": same id, `permanent` + email, `status invited`, `joined_at` cleared, the coordinator row closed `became_employee`), `member_invite()` / `member_deactivate()` (a coordinator with an active freelancer is `CONFLICT`; `comp_leave_grant()`; `app.absent_check()`; `app.end_day_reminder_due()`; `attendance_today_detail()`; `month_summary()`), `workload_warning_threshold` default 4 and set to 4. **`20260929053634_tasks_schema`**: the five enums, `task_types` (seeded per organization by `app.seed_org_task_types()` + backfill; - **Rules for this run (owner, 2026-09-29):** migrations stay expand-only (production holds real data; the staging database is shared by `main` and the phase previews); no CI polling loops (one check when a run is likely done; the orchestrator dispatches each unit's full CI run through the GitHub API, since the cloud session has no `gh`); no full e2e run across 18:30 UTC (midnight IST); commit and push at every unit boundary with a handoff; **PR #22** (tap feedback, pull-to-refresh, speed) merges into `main` during the phase: before the review, merge `origin/main` into `phase-4`, and every new screen follows its patterns (ARCHITECTURE §14.1 "Every tap is acknowledged", §14.2 i, §19); stop before the merge and the tags (pushed from the laptop); PR #21 (kickoff 5) stays unmerged until phase 4 is reviewed.
- **Odd, for the record:** the account's Fable limit cut the 4A builder at ~05:36 UTC; it resumed at 09:22 with the tree intact. The container restarted meanwhile: **Docker must be started by hand** (`nohup dockerd >/tmp/dockerd.log 2>&1 &`; the Supabase containers come back on their own) and the container ran ~50% slower afterwards. **`pnpm test:e2e -- <files>` runs the whole suite** (pnpm forwards the literal `--`); `pnpm test:e2e <files>` filters. The unit's diff is `1571299..HEAD` (the local `main` was stale until the orchestrator reset it to `origin/main`). A stale `next-server` from an e2e run can linger: stop it before the next run. **`pkill -f <pattern>` kills the calling shell when its own command line contains the pattern** (and `next-server` also matches Supabase Studio's container, which restarts itself): stop a leftover app server by PID.

## Things the next session must know
- **Release plan (owner, 2026-09-30, at the phase 4 merge):** phase 4 gets the tag `phase-4-done` only and **no release of its own**. **`v1.2.0` = phase 4 + 5A (the notification core) + the "Crew" label, released together** once all three are on `main`: production (which still holds only `v1.1.0`'s migrations) then applies phase 4's and 5A's migrations **in date order in one plain `supabase db push`** (`20260930050132` → `20260930070616` → `20260930073623` → phase 5's reconciling migration), so no migration lands out of order and `deploy.yml`'s production step needs no `--include-all`. The go-live runbook's pending staff invites and guide rebuild wait for that release (`docs/runbooks/go-live.md`, owner decision 2026-09-30). **Never tag a release from `main` while it holds phase 4 without 5A.**
- **Staging migrations with two open phase branches (2026-09-30, owner request during the phase 4 finish; `scripts/staging-migrations.sh`, used by `deploy.yml`'s staging job and `preview.yml`'s `staging migrations` job):** the Preview run on `phase-4` `4c3b8b1` failed at "staging migrations" ("Remote migration versions not found in local migrations directory: 20260930050132 20260930073623", `phase-5`'s 5A migrations already on staging), so `20260930070616_phase4_review_fixes` never reached staging. The script now applies whatever staging is missing even while staging holds another branch's versions: empty stand-ins for those versions in a scratch copy of `supabase/`, then `supabase migration up --include-all` (the CLI applies and records the missing files in version order), then a second listing; never `migration repair`, never an edit of an applied migration. Proved locally on a copy of staging's state (phase-4 through `task_reads` + phase-5's two): reproduced the CLI error, then the script applied and recorded only `20260930070616`, a second run skipped, the dry run agreed. **Consequence on staging, now:** `phase4_review_fixes` and phase-5's `notifications_core` both re-create `task_create`, `task_update_assignment`, `task_submit_done`, `task_review` and `task_set_approver`; staging now runs phase-4's versions (without phase-5's `app.notify` calls), so **the phase-5 preview sends no notifications from those five until phase 5 adds a migration of its own** (the job warns naming both files). **Phase 5 must, when it merges `main` (after phase 4):** its `20260930050132` sorts before phase-4's `20260930070616`, so (a) on a fresh database (CI, local) `070616` runs after `050132` and drops the notification calls from those five functions: phase 5 needs a new migration, sorting after both, that re-creates them with both the phase-4 review fixes (decisions 33–36) and the notifications; (b) **production: settled by the release plan (owner, 2026-09-30)**: phase 4 is not released alone; `v1.2.0` carries phase 4 + 5A + the "Crew" label, so production applies `050132`, `070616`, `073623` and the reconciling migration in date order in one plain `db push` (a release of phase 4 alone would have made `050132` older than production's newest, which a plain push refuses). Nothing in production was touched here.
- **Task page rework mechanics (Kickoff 4 decisions 26–32; read before touching the task page, its views, Chat, `task_reads` or the task specs):** (1) **The views are client state in one page**, not routes: every panel is rendered with the page and shown by CSS on `data-view` (`PANEL` in `task-view.tsx`), so the first paint is right before hydration and a switch costs no request; the URL follows through `history.replaceState` **after `closeOverlaysThen`** (a closed sheet leaves a spent overlay entry; writing the URL on it lost the view on the next back, the 3.4 lesson). Next keys the page without its search params, so a replace never remounts it; `useState` holds the view, so a refresh, an action's answer or a revalidation never switches it (decision 32). (2) **Details is one element** placed by CSS: on a phone the main column is `max-md:contents` so the title bar and the views' bar stick for the whole page (a sticky element inside a wrapper would only stick inside the wrapper), and Details follows the panels; from `md` up it is the sticky right column. (3) **Chat's thread is drawn in one place at a time:** inline until `useIsDesktop()` says phone, then only inside the sheet; the draft lives in `TaskChat`, outside the sheet and the lazy composer, so a back keeps it. A phone arriving on `?tab=chat` opens the sheet once (render-time state, not an effect: `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect`). The sheet's keyboard placement is inline style (`bottom` = the keyboard's inset, `height` = the visible height minus 0.75rem), because the Sheet's own `data-[side=bottom]:bottom-0` outranks a plain utility. (4) **Marking read** runs only when something is unread, up to the newest comment's own time (never `now()`, so a comment written meanwhile stays new), in the background (no Retry, a failed mark leaves the count), and revalidates `/tasks` (layout) and `/approvals` so a back to a list drops the marker. (5) **The step dialogs live once, in `TaskNextStep`,** opened from the bar or from ⋯ through the provider's `dialog`; the Done sheet mounts on its first open (render-time `setDoneLoaded`) and stays mounted. (6) **`data-view-tab`, not `data-tab`,** on the view buttons: `data-tab` is the bottom bar's pre-hydration attribute. The views' bar sets `data-live` when it hydrates (`markLive`): **a spec waits for it before tapping a view** (a tap before hydration is lost: found in `tasks.spec` at 375px). (7) **Specs:** `getByLabel("Comment")` also matches the "Comments" list; use `getByRole("textbox", { name: "Comment" })`. Read a task id from `new URL(page.url()).pathname` (the address may carry `?tab=`). The keyboard is stood in by an own `height` property on `window.visualViewport` plus a `resize` event (`openKeyboard` / `closeKeyboard` in `task-page.spec`). A sheet is measured after `animationsSettled`. The held task-page fixtures use a short title (`shortTag`): the title bar is traced and a long title wraps on a phone; the title skeleton sits in the h1's line box (24px, 32px from `md`). `task-page.spec` uses `tasks.spec`'s people on days 31–40 out (never a day `tasks.spec`'s warnings count). (8) **Screens for the phone walk (installed, 375 and 430, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** the task page for the Owner, an approving Admin, a Staff assignee and a coordinator: the title bar with ⋯; the glance line (state dot, flag and priority, relative deadline, red when overdue) and the needed line; the next-step bar (Task Noted, Start work, Mark done / again, "for Asha", Approve and Request changes) and it stepping aside with the keyboard; the views' bar sticky under the title bar and scrolling at 200%; **Work** (brief, stages with Add stage and Remove stage, the hand-in first once handed in, its links, the late reason), **Chat** (the full-height sheet, the thread on its newest comment, your own on the right, "Writing as" for a coordinator, the composer above the keyboard, Send, back closes it, "Chat · 2 new", arriving on `?tab=chat`), **Activity** (five rows, Show all, a collapsed tick line), **Details** (people with Noted, client, type, approval route, created by, fields); ⋯ (Mark done, Decide it yourself, Edit, Change approver, Reopen, Cancel) and each dialog; the loading screen of each view (`?tab=activity`, `?tab=details`, `?tab=chat`); the desktop's two columns; the unread bubble on the Tasks tab rows (each role), All tasks (cards, table) and Approvals' task rows; full names in the Attendance, Leave, Extra work and Expenses approval rows and dialogs, comp leave's dialogs and the /me welcome. View controls added: the task page's views; Show all.
- **4C mechanics (read before touching the task lists, Approvals' task group, the badges, People's freelancer screens, Settings → Task types or Templates, Suggested tasks or the 4C specs):** (1) **The directory is cheap for every role since `894a098`** (`app.directory_visible_ids()`, decision 21): the lists read `member_directory` whole (`src/app/(app)/tasks/reads.ts`, one `cache()`d read per request, shared with the dialog's setup); 4B mechanics (1)'s "read Staff's by ids" is history. (2) **The badges stream** (`NavCount`, ARCHITECTURE §19): the `(app)` layout starts `task_counts()` and the Owner's attendance counts and never awaits them; awaiting them made fast pages lose the race with their loading screen. (3) **The task dialog has three modes** (`TaskFormMode`: `create`, `convert`, `edit`). `convert` starts from a `ConvertedRequest` (title, details as the description, the label and, for the Owner only, `approverId`: the label's Admin, computed on the server from `readLabelClients()`, the same cached read the setup uses) and saves through `convertRequest` → `task_request_convert` (task_create's arguments after `request_id`). **The draft carries the template** (`TaskDraft.templateId`, "" for none): the form itself remounts on "Discard?" → Keep editing, so anything that must survive a back lives in the draft, outside the `Dialog`. An approver the setup no longer offers (a client's Admin who left) is treated as nobody (`approverId` in `TaskForm`). (4) **"Discard?" on a dialog form** is one pattern (the task dialog, the Suggest dialog, the template dialog): the draft's state sits in the component that renders the `Dialog`, `phase: "form" | "discard"`, the `Dialog` open only in `form`, a `ConfirmDialog` ("Discard …" / Keep editing) in `discard`; Cancel and back both go through `requestClose`; a save closes directly. The task types dialog (a name and a kind) has none (Ideas). (5) **Dialogs load on first open** (`next/dynamic`, `ssr: false`): `TaskFormDialog`, `SuggestTaskDialog`, `TemplateDialog`; with the template dialog in its first load `/settings/templates` was 843.1 KB (now 791.7). `/today` and `/my-day` stand at 783.5 of 785 KB: nothing new may reach the shell. (6) **Templates are a plain edit** (RLS + `app.task_templates_guard()`, audited): the service role cannot insert one for someone else (the guard wants `created_by = auth.uid()` outside a transition), so a spec makes a template through the screen or as that person; `removeTemplatesNamed` nulls `tasks.template_id` first. A template's field defaults go through `validateCustomFieldsFor(…, { skipRequired: true })`. (7) **Requests:** RLS decides who sees a request and so who may decide it (`app.task_request_visible`), **except their own** (Kickoff 4 decision 23: `task_request_convert` / `_decline` refuse an Admin's own; `requestActions` offers them Withdraw only); `countRequestsToDecide(viewerId)` counts the pending ones the viewer sees and did not make. "Open the task" follows the task's RLS (`listTaskRequests` embeds `task:tasks!task_id(id)`, null when the viewer may not see it; `opensTask`). An Admin suggests with their own clients only (`readLabelClients()` feeds their Suggest dialog; `task_request_create` refuses another Admin's client, 4C review S8a). (8) **The 4C specs' people and fixtures** (all per project, so the projects run side by side, each file serial): `task-lists.spec` `list-{staff,coord,admin}-<project>` and the freelancers `40000000-…-4..6`; `freelancers.spec` `people-coord[2]-<project>` (the Owner's storage state; a deactivated coordinator is put back in `finally`); `task-settings.spec` flows on the desktop project only (the type order is one shared list); `task-requests.spec` `req-staff-<project>`, `list-admin-<project>`, `task-admin-<project>` as the other Admin (read only), `list-staff-<project>` as another suggester, the client fixture `Req client <project>` (run by `list-admin`). Helpers: `removeRequestsTitled`, `removeTemplatesNamed`, `removeTaskTypesNamed`, `removeFreelancersNamed`. **Gotchas:** `getByLabel("Stage 1")` also matches "Remove stage 1" (use `exact: true`); People shows ten cards at a time on a phone, so a spec opens a person by address; **a loading screen is held by its data, never its document** (4C review M1, `e2e/loading-screens.spec.ts`, which replaced `mobile.spec`'s `LOADING_SCREENS` and `heldShell`): the e2e build and server reach Supabase through `e2e/hold-proxy.ts` (Playwright's first `webServer`, `127.0.0.1:54331`; CI's build step points `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` at it); a test signs its page in with a session of its own (`ownSession`: GoTrue's password grant written into the `sb-127-auth-token` cookie as `@supabase/ssr` writes it) and holds that session's reads of one PostgREST path only the **page** reads (`holdReads(path, session)`; never one the `(app)` layout awaits, or no shell streams), so the page cannot answer before its loading screen and no other test is ever held; the hold is the open connection (release in `finally`; a dying test releases it). Each entry names its marker, its held path and the points its skeleton traces (`{ held, settled }` selectors: the first heading, the first row), checked within 1px after the release, at every width (the desktop project runs it too), plus a per-test fixture when the settled page needs data to show them (a Staff task, a suggestion, a template, a task waiting for the Admin's check). Held paths: Owner `/today` `rpc/attendance_today_detail`, Admin `/today` `extra_work_notes` (the strip's End day reads the overtime note: the seeded Admin's day must be started and not ended, asserted first), `/me` `coordinated_freelancers`, the task screens and an Admin's Approvals `tasks`, Suggested tasks `task_requests`, Task types `task_types`, Templates `task_templates`. **A skeleton passes the page's `description` and `help`** (4C review S7; the copy in one module both import: `settings/headers.ts`, a route's `copy.ts`, `standInDescription`) and sits each bar in its text's line box (`flex h-5 items-center` for a `text-sm` line). **The hold proxy passes WebSocket upgrades and streamed answers untouched** (owner requirement 2026-09-30; a byte-for-byte tunnel, never held; proved by `tests/hold-proxy.test.ts` in `pnpm check`): **phase 5 (5A) must add an e2e check that a Realtime subscription connects through the proxy once the bell lands, and must start Realtime in CI's e2e job (remove `realtime` from its `pnpm supabase start -x …` list).** **Never set `data-slot` on a primitive whose slot the CSS keys on** (`select-trigger`: 44px and 16px; `*dialog-content`, `sheet-content`: overscroll containment): the checkpoint's "Start from" trigger measured 32px. (9) **Screens for the phone walk (4C; installed, 375 and 430, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** **Tasks** for the Owner and an Admin ("Needs you" rows with their reason lines, "N tasks waiting for your approval" into Approvals, "N suggested tasks to decide" into Suggested tasks, Open tasks, "See all …", "Suggested tasks"; New task) and for Staff ("My tasks": Not noted, Changes requested, Due today, Upcoming, Overdue, "For Asha" rows, "N tasks with the reviewers", All my tasks, Your suggestions, the Suggest a task FAB), each skeleton; **the badges** on the bar, the More cell and sheet, the sidebar; **`/tasks/all`** (search, the state / deadline / person / client / type / engagement filters as select sheets: view controls, cards, the skeleton; Staff's "All my tasks"); **Approvals → Tasks** (Approve and its Undo, Review with the hand-in's links, Request changes… with its reason, Approve all N and its question); **People** (Add person with Employee / Freelancer and the Coordinator picker, "Freelancer · with Ravi" in the list, a freelancer's page with no tabs, the Coordinator card with history and reasons, Change coordinator, ⋯ Invite as employee with its warning and the invite link, Deactivate a coordinator with the handover picker, Reactivate a freelancer with the coordinator picker); **/me** "Your freelancers"; **Settings → Task types** (the list with ↑ ↓, the ⋯ sheet on a phone, Add with the kind and the event switches, Edit, the archive question, Archived with Restore; an Admin has no row); **Settings → Custom fields → Tasks** ("Applies to": every task or one type, the type groups); **Settings → Templates** (the list with type · priority · stages · author, Add template FAB, the Add / Edit dialog with its selects, stages and field defaults, "Discard this template?", the ⋯ sheet, the archive question, Archived with Restore); **Suggested tasks** (Waiting with Make it a task / Decline… / Withdraw, Decided with the outcome, the reason and "Open the task", the empty state, the skeleton; the Suggest a task dialog with its client picker and "Discard this suggestion?", Make it a task (the task dialog started from the suggestion), the Decline reason, the Withdraw question); **New task**'s Start from picker, and "Discard this task?" keeping the template. View controls added: `/tasks/all`'s filters and search.
- **4B mechanics (read before touching the task dialog, the task page, `member_directory` reads or the task specs):** (1) **Never read the whole `member_directory` for someone without `team.view`:** for them `app.directory_visible()` walks the organization's tasks for every member row, measured **2 s for 82 members and 33 tasks** (2026-09-29; production will have more of both). The task page reads the Owner's and Admins' directory whole (15 ms) and Staff's by the ids on the screen (`listDirectoryOf(ids)` in `modules/team`, 1 ms: the id filter reaches the index under the view), in a second wave after the task's own reads (`loadNames` in `src/app/(app)/tasks/[id]/task.ts`). 4C's "My tasks" must do the same (or rewrite `directory_visible()` first, Ideas). The first symptom was a server action's transition that stayed "pending" past a test's 5 s: the action's revalidation re-renders the page, and that render took 4 s. (2) **The dialog's draft lives outside the `Dialog`** (`TaskFormDialog` holds `phase: "form" | "discard"`): back or ✕ with changes closes the sheet and opens the "Discard?" confirmation; Keep editing reopens the sheet with the draft; the overlay controller counts the reopen as a new layer. **Every Dialog, AlertDialog and Sheet keys its portal by its opening** (`core/ui/primitives/modal-opening.ts`, `49e39ce`): Radix portals a modal's overlay (100 ms exit) and its content (200 ms on a phone) separately, and a reopen inside that gap mounted the new overlay after the still-closing content, over it. After a save the `leaving` ref makes the close that `closeOverlaysThen` causes ask nothing, then the new task is pushed (`NAV_FORWARD`) or the edit sheet closes. (3) **The warning check** runs 250 ms after the people or the days change (`loadAvailability`, keyed `"<ids>|<days>"`); `checking` disables Create until the answer for the current key is in, and the warnings are recomputed on every render from that answer (`assignmentWarnings`). (4) **Long forms in a sheet:** the footer is sticky (`sticky bottom-[calc(-1rem-var(--app-safe-bottom))] md:static` on `DialogFooter`), and **the form needs `min-w-0`**: `MODAL_SURFACE` is a grid, and a nowrap name (a truncated person row, a select's value) set the column's minimum and widened the sheet past a 375px phone at 130–200% text. `MODAL_SURFACE`'s `duration-200` carries the default `transition-property: all`, so after a root font change the sheet's rem padding eases for 200 ms while the footer's negative margin jumps: a check measured mid-transition sees the footer 3px past the edge; the task spec waits for `getAnimations({ subtree: true })` before measuring. (5) **An action button with a name in it** ("Mark done for Test Task Freelancer (desktop)") needs `h-auto min-h-11 py-2 whitespace-normal` (`ACTION` in `task-work-actions.tsx`): `Button` is `whitespace-nowrap`. (6) **A stage tick's audit row names no stage** (the diff holds only `done_at`, `done_by`, `on_behalf_of`; `entity_id` is the task): the history names it when the stage is still ticked from that instant (`tickedStages` by epoch ms) and says "a stage" otherwise. Since the 4B review (S5) the guard stamps `done_at = now()` on every tick, whatever the app sends (a non-null value only says "tick"), so the row and its audit entry carry the same server time. **Naming the Owner (S8 interim):** a Staff viewer's directory may not hold the Owner, so the entries only the Owner writes (`completed`, `approver_changed`, `changes_requested` at `step = owner`) and the first card's change request (`reviewerName`) say "The Owner" instead of "Someone"; everyone else hidden stays "Someone" (an owner question, Ideas "4B later items (review)"). (7) **`e2e/tasks.spec.ts` runs in parallel**: each test names its tasks with its own prefix (`removeTasksTitled(prefix)` in `e2e/helpers.ts` removes them and their children first) and uses its own IST weekday (`workingDay(n)`, Mon–Fri, never 2 Oct, the settings spec's holiday), because `member_availability()` counts every open task of a person; the seed people are per project; arrange with `rpcAs(..., "task_create" | "task_submit_done" | "task_update_assignment")` and `taskTypeId(name)`. No spec may save a workload threshold other than 4 (the warning test runs beside `settings.spec`). The flows skip 430px (`viewport.width === 430`; describe-level skips get fixtures, not the project); the installed and large-text tests run at 375 and 430. (8) **Commit buttons added, for PR #22's merge** (every `primary` / `submit` `Button` needs `pending` and `pendingLabel` there): `task-form-dialog.tsx` **Create task** / **Save changes** ("Creating…" / "Saving…"); `task-work-actions.tsx` **Task Noted** / "Noted for Asha" (the first is `primary`) and the Done sheet's **Mark done** ("Marking done…"); `task-stages.tsx` **Add stage** ("Adding…"); `task-comment-button.tsx` **Post comment** ("Posting…"); `task-menu.tsx` **Save approver** ("Saving…"); the `ConfirmDialog` / `ReasonDialog` commits (Approve task, Request changes, Remove <Admin> as approver, Remove stage, Cancel task, Reopen task, Discard task / Discard changes) take their working label from PR #22's `workingLabel`; **New task** is a `strong` trigger (`pending: none`); the thresholds form's Save already existed. **The merge list (4B review S12), for the PR #22 merge:** `src/app/(app)/tasks/[id]/page.tsx` (the reads awaited after `requirePermission`) will trip the parallel-reads sweep: mark it `perf: sequential` with the reason (`startEarly` would start Staff's 2 s whole-directory read before the session says who they are, mechanics (1)); `useAction(…, { creates: true })` for `createTask`, `addTaskComment` and `addTaskStage`; **Start work**, "Start for Asha" and "Noted for Asha" need `commits` and pending labels ("Starting…", "Noting…"), and the stage tick is disabled offline; check `ACTION` (`task-work-actions.tsx`, wrapping labels) against PR #22's fixed-width pending label. **Lazy since the 4B fixes:** the Done sheet (`task-done-dialog.tsx`), the comment sheet (`task-comment-dialog.tsx`), Add a stage (`task-stage-dialog.tsx`) and Change approver (`task-approver-dialog.tsx`) are `next/dynamic` imports, mounted on their first open and kept mounted after it (a draft survives a back); `TaskMenu` itself stays in the first load (it draws the header's ⋯ at first paint, and its own code is 2.8 KB: measured by building the page without it). `bundle-budget.json` budgets `/tasks` (815 KB) and `/tasks/[id]` (835 KB). The pressed style: the only raw clickable element added is `LinkedText`'s `<a>` (`task-link`), which needs `pressable-row`; everything else is `Button`, `DropdownMenuItem`, `SelectItem`, `BackLink` or a `label` around a radio / checkbox. **Applied at the `main` merge (2026-09-29, `b4fbc40`):** `pending` + `pendingLabel` through `useAction` on Create task / Save changes ("Creating…" / "Saving…"), Mark done ("Marking done…", "Marking done for Asha…"), Post comment ("Posting…"), Add stage ("Adding…") and Save approver ("Saving…"), each with `ActionStatus` above its footer; the confirmations keep `workingLabel` ("Approving task…", "Requesting changes…", "Cancelling task…"); `creates: true` for `createTask`, `addTaskComment` and `addTaskStage`; Task Noted, "Noted for Asha", Start work and "Start for Asha" are `commits` with "Noting…" / "Starting…" (one `useAction` for the row: the tapped button shows its label, the others wait, one `ActionStatus` under them; Retry is safe, the functions refuse a second note or start); the form reads its inputs when the action runs (`send`, hoisted), so an edit's Retry sends what is on screen; **the stage tick** runs through `useAction`, is disabled offline (`useOnline`, `aria-describedby` the banner) and is held with `useOptimistic` until the saved stages arrive (`useAction`'s pending ends when the action answers, before the router re-renders: without it the box flipped back for a frame); `LinkedText`'s links have `pressable-row`; the task page starts its reads with `startEarly(reads)` (no `perf: sequential` needed: they really start before the check; the names still wait for the role). `ACTION`'s wrapping labels hold with the grid-cell pending label (the large-text spec is green at 130% and 200%). The task page is a drill-down, so it has no pull-to-refresh (§14.2 i); `/tasks` is a tab root and has it. The budgets stay 815 / 835 KB (784.7 / 801.6 KB after the merge: PR #22's shell adds ~10 KB to every route). **Seed ids after the merge:** both sides had used 059–063; the task people keep 059–073, the pull and tap people are 074–078 (their specs' `IDS` follow), and the three freelancers are `40000000-…-00000000000{1,2,3}` (`scripts/measure-seed.sql` owns `3000000K-…`, its members `30000000-…-<n>`). (9) **Screens for the phone walk (4B; installed, 375 and 430, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** Tasks for the Owner and an Admin (the stand-in with the New task FAB, its skeleton) and for Staff (no FAB); the **create dialog** (the Type, Priority, Add a person, Client label and Checked first by select sheets; a person row with the primary radio, the Freelancer mark, ✕ and its warnings; the deadline date and time; the event fields for Shoot / Meeting / Posting; description; stages; a task custom field; the sticky Create task; "Discard this task?" on back); the **task page** for the Owner, the approving Admin, a Staff assignee and a coordinator (the first card and its buttons, the Done sheet with the late reason, the Approve confirmation, Request changes, Decide it yourself, the hand-in link, stages with Add stage and Remove stage, people, details, comments and the Add a comment sheet with "Writing as", the history, the skeleton), the **⋯ menu** (the edit dialog and "Discard your changes?", Change approver, Reopen, Cancel), the not-found for a task someone may not see; **Settings → Thresholds** (the workload warning) and **Settings → Custom fields → Tasks** (the Owner's five tabs in two rows, an Admin's three). View controls added: none (the task page has no tabs; the Custom fields tabs gained one).
- **4A mechanics (read before touching members, tasks, or any pgTAP teardown):** (1) **Every pgTAP file that deletes members deletes the task tables and `member_coordinators` first** (the 4A block before `delete from public.members;`), and a file that deletes the organization deletes `task_types` before its last `activity_log` delete (its audit rows name the organization; `02`). A new task-side table joins that block in every file. (2) **The on-behalf contract** (`app.task_actor(task_id, on_behalf_of)`): the caller holds `tasks.work`, is `permanent` and an active assignee; or `on_behalf_of` is an active `freelance` assignee whose `app.coordinator_of()` is the caller at that moment. A freelancer's own id as `auth.uid()` is `FORBIDDEN` (their row is active, so `current_member()` would otherwise answer), a former coordinator and any other member are `FORBIDDEN`, naming oneself is `VALIDATION`. Comments and stage ticks through the API use `app.task_on_behalf_ok()` in their guards instead. The audit rows carry `on_behalf_of_id` when a transition function sets the override's `on_behalf_of` key; since the 4A review (S2) the API paths carry it too: `audit_row_change()` falls back to the row's own `on_behalf_of` on an insert (a comment) or an update that changes `on_behalf_of` / `done_at` (a tick), never on a rename, an untick or a delete. (3) **The API guards are `security invoker`** (`app.task_comments_guard`, `app.task_stages_guard`, like `list_items_owner_guard`): a `security definer` trigger runs as postgres, so `app.in_transition()` would be true and the guard would wave the API write through (caught by pgTAP 32 on the first run). The scope helpers they call are security definer themselves. (4) **`tasks` has no API write at all**: every change is a `task_*` function; `task_update_assignment(task_id, changes jsonb, warnings)` is the one edit door (keys: title, description, task_type_id, client_id, priority, due_at, event_date, event_start_at, event_end_at, location, purpose, custom_fields, reminder_rules, assignee_ids (the full set), primary_owner_id); it returns the changed keys and raises `VALIDATION` "Nothing changed" otherwise, so 4B's form sends only what differs. (5) **The Owner never decides at the Admin step:** `task_review` on `submitted` is `INVALID_STATE` for the Owner; `task_set_approver(task_id, null)` sends the task to the Owner (`admin_step none`), an assignee-Admin named as approver skips the step. **So the Owner's override of the Admin step is two taps in 4.4 / 4.5:** set the approver to none (`task_set_approver(id, null)`), then approve (`task_review(id, 'approved')`). (6) **Event tasks:** `event_date` is required for `kind = event`, refused otherwise; `event_start_at` must fall on that IST date; `purpose` only on an event; `location` only when the type `has_location` (seeded: Shoot / Site Visit and Meeting yes, Posting no). A date-only event has no `event_start_at` and never overlaps. (7) **`member_availability(from_date, to_date, member_ids)`** is what 4.3's warnings read: `open_tasks_due` per IST day (open tasks whose `due_at` falls that day), `event_blocks` (`[{start_at, end_at}]`, no end = one hour), `leave` (`leave` / `half_day` / `comp_leave` / `requested`; null for a freelancer), `present` (today only); 62 days at most; the Owner is never listed. (8) **A freelancer's row looks like Staff to the existing screens** (`role = staff`, `status active`, `joined_at` set): `hasAttendance()` in `modules/team/domain` would offer the Owner the Leave / Attendance / Month tabs on `/people/[id]` for a freelancer, and the People card shows no mark yet; 4C's People work adds `engagement` to `TeamMember` (read `member_directory.engagement`) and hides those tabs. No freelancer can be created through the UI before 4C, so nothing is broken today. (9) **`CurrentMember.email` is `string | null`** since `members.email` is nullable (a session always belongs to an employee, whose row has one); `/me` renders it as before. (10) **Freelancer → employee needs the auth user first:** the 4C action calls `auth.admin.createUser({ id: memberId, email, email_confirm: false })` (GoTrue accepts an explicit id) and then `member_invite_employee(member_id, email)`, then issues the link as `issueInviteToken()` does; the function checks the auth user under that id carries the address. (11) **`task_create` positions stages with `app.next_position()`** (`a0`, `a1`, …), the SQL mirror of `core/lists nextPosition()`; the API inserts by 4B send their own `position`. (12) **The `directory_visible()` task branch** calls `app.task_visible()` per task of the organization for each directory row a Staff member reads; fine at Pixora's size, an index-friendly rewrite is under Ideas if the People screen ever slows for Staff. (13) **After the 4A review fixes** (`20260929104507_phase4a_review_fixes`): **recorded warnings are `availability.view`'s** (a task's history shows `warning_overridden` entries to the Owner and Admins only; 4B's dialog computes warnings for the creator anyway); **`member_coordinators` and its activity entries are `team.view`'s**: 4C's People page reads the table (reason included) for the Owner and Admins, and "your freelancers" on /me reads **`coordinated_freelancers`** (the caller's own rows, current and past, no reason) with names from `member_directory`; a Staff query on `member_coordinators` returns nothing; **`app.task_check_fields` takes a 12th argument** `p_client_changed` (true in `task_create`, "client_id sent and different" in `task_update_assignment`); **a coordinator's deactivation closes the rows of deactivated freelancers pointing at them**, so 4C's reactivate flow for a freelancer sets a coordinator first whenever `member_reactivate()` answers `INVALID_STATE`.
- **Phase 3c review decisions (2026-09-29, `/review-phase 3c`, Fable 5.1, cloud session):** the architecture and the security review of `main...phase-3c` found **no must-fix**; the exit-criteria audit passed. Fixes made, four commits: **(1) `d5c281f` the Owner's comp leave uses a credit** (rule (b) of the review's two options: `attendance_decide(correct, comp_leave)` and `leave_owner_edit(…, comp_leave)` draw the member's free credits valid on that date oldest first and use them at once, or refuse the whole call; **why:** kickoff 3b decision 16 says approval consumes a credit and the date must be on or before the credit's expiry, decisions 12, 14 and 15 make the Owner the one who grants, and the two docs lines that said Owner-set comp leave carries no credit were a 3b.2 deferral, since rewritten; **the limits:** a full day only on these routes (½ stays `leave_submit_comp`'s), an Owner edit into comp leave is one date, never a day off; migration `20260929020230_owner_comp_leave_credit` with `app.comp_credit_draw`, pgTAP `30` (62), `07` and `00` updated, the Correct and Owner-edit dialogs grey "Comp leave (no credit)" out where the balance is known, Approvals shows the refusal; "3bA mechanics (5)"). **(2) `6b1f6bd` the stand-in skeletons trace the stand-ins** (owner decision; `StandInSkeleton`, line counts measured at 375 / 430 / 464+; "3cB mechanics (2)"). **(3) `f4ea2f8` ops:** `scripts/smoke-deploy.sh` gains a fourth block (the Continue page's `no-store`, `noindex`, `no-referrer` on `GET /auth/confirm`, proved against a local `next start`); pgTAP `29` gains the Admin (VALIDATION) and the Owner (FORBIDDEN) on `leave_request_change(…, comp_leave)` (34); the restore runbook never puts the password on a command line (`PGPASSWORD` read with `read -s`, `-e PGPASSWORD` on the client containers, a key=value target); the go-live runbook and the restore runbook say the production drill ends with the pg_cron WARNING and "restore verified, with 1 warning(s)"; `backup.yml`'s comment, README "Backups" step 4 and ARCHITECTURE §17 state the trust model honestly (the `postgres` role reads and writes; whoever can run the workflow holds a full database credential; no reviewer by design); `Remove-Variable secure` closes the bootstrap block; the `sslmode=verify-full` step is the owner's at the first production backup run (README step 5, the runbook, Ideas "3cA later items (f)"). **(4) this commit, docs:** the lock-order paragraph (DATA-MODEL §3: `attendance_start_day()` takes `leave:` first, pgTAP `09`; `app.end_not_recorded()`), the stale 2.x sentences in WORKFLOWS §1, ARCHITECTURE §5 and PERMISSIONS §3, the ADR-0012 dated amendment for the Continue page. **The owner's notes honoured:** the Continue page reviewed as built (no change to the URL, the template or the bootstrap link); no PDF committed (both rebuilt on the laptop after the merge); no full e2e run across 18:30 UTC; the merge, then STOP: the tags (`phase-3c-done`, `v1.0.0`) are pushed from the laptop. **Backfill evidence for the contract migration:** Preview run 36431544216 applied `20260928131234_contract_phase3b.sql` to the staging database at 13:51 UTC on 2026-09-28, a database holding rows, without error; the `backfilled` row count is one owner query on staging (`select count(*) from activity_log where action = 'backfilled'`). **Phone walk (2026-09-29, owner):** the installed phase-3c preview on the owner's phone (gesture navigation, both themes, 130% and 200% text), every screen the phase added or changed (the stand-ins for every role and their skeletons, the Settings "Coming soon" rows, the Continue page, the Owner's comp-leave Correct and Edit dialogs): **all passed**.. **Merge:** PR #23, merge commit `dbf8242` (2026-09-29); the tags `phase-3c-done` and `v1.0.0` pushed from the laptop; staging deployed by Deploy run 36524965574, the production deploy (run 36525143120) waiting for the owner's approval. **(5) the CI red on /me (fixed after the docs commit, `fix(ui): 3c review: skeletons fit at 200% text on a phone`), two gotchas:** **a fixed-rem skeleton bar (`w-40`, `w-52`) inside a flex or grid column needs `min-w-0` on the column**, or the bar's rem width becomes the column's minimum and `max-w-full` clamps to nothing at 200% system text (12rem is 384px, more than a 375px phone's 311px of content); a row the page wraps (`flex-wrap`, `flex-[1_1_10rem]`, `shrink-0` on the button) wraps in its skeleton too. And in e2e, **`page.goto` resolves on `load`, which a streamed page's reveal can follow** (React 19.2 batches `$RC` reveals up to 300 ms after the previous one), so a sweep of the settled page waits until no `[data-slot="skeleton"]` is left (`expectSettled`) and a loading screen is checked by holding it (`heldShell`), never by timing; a soft navigation's loading screen cannot be held from a test (it comes from the router's prefetch cache, whose state a test cannot see: three attempts, all flaky at 3 workers).
- **3bB mechanics (read before touching expenses, End day's question, the month summary or Reports):** (1) **Money isolation:** `expense_claims` is named only in `modules/expenses/data` (lint `EXPENSE_SELECTORS`, fixture `denied-expense.ts`) and `core/db` types; RLS own + `expenses.decide`; activity entries readable by the claimant (`activity_log_select_expenses_self`) and `activity.view_all`; a receipt by its uploader and `expenses.decide` (`app.file_visible` branch); the Approvals list and badge query claims only for `expenses.decide`. pgTAP `26` proves every role, including an Admin after the decisions. No notification text exists yet; 5.1 must keep amounts out of push text (decision 9). (2) **The End-day question is a cross-module follow-up** (`core/ui/composites/follow-up.tsx`, ARCHITECTURE §3.1): the page hands `<EndDayClaims>` (expenses) to `TodayAttendanceStrip`, which wraps itself in `FollowUpHost`; `EndDayButton` asks only inside a host and calls `useFollowUpTrigger()(endsOn)` after a successful end. **The host holds the state** because the refresh after End day removes End day's button (the first version kept the state in the button and the form never opened: caught by e2e). The claim form is a `next/dynamic` import (budget: `/my-day` and `/today` 772.4 KB of 785). (3) **Receipts:** purpose `receipt` (PNG/JPEG/WebP ≤ 10 MB, `attendance.self`), uploaded with a preview before the claim; `expense_claim_submit` repeats `files_reference_guard`'s checks itself (a security definer function passes the guard), and a failed claim reuses the upload on retry. `receipt_file_id` is a FK, so the orphan cleanup spares attached receipts. (4) **Amounts:** the form parses text (`parseAmount`); the schemas check two decimals with a tolerance (`hasAtMostTwoDecimals`: `150.1 * 100` is not an integer in floating point; review must-fix). (5) **Claim window** `claimWindow()` mirrors `app.expense_window_start()` (this IST month, last month through the 5th, never a future date). (6) **Month summary** (`month_summary()`, pgTAP `27`): only decided days (approved | corrected) count; `pending_review` days are "N days waiting for your review"; past `awaiting_choice` days are counted nowhere (Ideas); a half day with `credit_days` is comp leave, never additional leave; day-off rows are excluded from leave/half/absent; working days use today's calendar (Ideas); members = roles with `attendance.self`, joined by the month's end, not deactivated before its start (**phase 4 adds `engagement = 'permanent'`**). The expense line is `modules/expenses` beside it (claims **dated in that month**, approved and unpaid). (7) **Screens:** `/people/[id]/month?month=yyyy-MM` (tab key shared through `routeKey`), `/reports` (Owner: a list with Month; Admin: the placeholder), `/reports/month?month=` (24 months back). The leave and person tab bars now use `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fit,minmax(min(100%,4.5rem),1fr))]`: four in a row at 100%, wrapping at large text. (8) **e2e:** `e2e/expenses.spec.ts` (`expense-<project>@`), `e2e/month-summary.spec.ts` (`month-<project>@`, days and claims arranged in last month through the service key); `resetExpenseClaims()` in helpers (also called by `removeFixturePerson`). Every pgTAP file that deletes members deletes `expense_claims` first. (9) **Screens 3bB added or changed, for the phase-3b phone walk (375 and 430, installed, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** Attendance & leave → Expenses tab (list, Add expense form with the category select sheet and the receipt photo picker, Withdraw confirmation), the End-day confirmation's "Any expenses to claim today?" and the claim form it opens (Add another, Done), Approvals → Expenses (review sheet with the receipt, Reject reason dialog), Settings → Expenses (categories, receipt amount), `/people/[id]` four tabs and the Month tab (summary, expenses, Mark all paid / Mark paid with the date), More → Reports and Reports → Month.
- **3bA mechanics (read before touching attendance, the strip, the prompt, extra work or comp leave):** (1) **Two apps, one staging database while the phase is open (decision 28):** `main` still runs the 2.2 gate (`attendance_touch()`, `session_logout()`, the `maxoff_day` cookie) and the 3b preview runs Start day. Both read the same rows: a 2.x gate choice is `pending_review` Present with no `started_at`, so the prompt stays away, `absent_check` never proposes it, and Start day on it only stamps `started_at`; the history (and 3b.4's month summary) read `first_login_at` / `last_logout_at` as start / end when `started_at` / `ended_at` are null (decision 31). (2) **Nothing is written by rendering:** `attendance_own_today()` only reads; the day exists once the person starts it or chooses leave. (3) **End day looks at yesterday only when today has no start** (3bA review must-fix: a second tap after today ended would have written a 33-hour day onto an open yesterday). The strip then reads "Yesterday not ended · started …" and its confirmation says "End yesterday's day?". (4) **The overtime note in End day is one transaction with the end:** a refused note refuses the end, so the strip hides "Worked late? Add an overtime note" when that day already has one (`hasOvertimeNote`). (5) **Comp leave only through `leave_submit_comp()`:** the member's plain request and change schemas take `leave | half_day` only (`MEMBER_LEAVE_TYPES`, zod-free in `domain/requests.ts`); the 2.x `leave_submit()` still takes `comp_leave` for `main`. A comp request cannot be changed, only cancelled and requested again. A half comp day is `type = half_day` with `credit_days = 0.5`: **3b.4 must not count it as additional leave** (decision 18: comp leave never counts). **The Owner's correction to comp leave and edit into comp leave draw a credit too** (3c review, 2026-09-29): `attendance_decide(correct, comp_leave)` and `leave_owner_edit(…, comp_leave)` set `credit_days = 1.0`, draw oldest first (`app.comp_credit_draw`) and use at once, or refuse with "Grant one first from their Leave tab"; a full day only, one date, never a day off (WORKFLOWS §2, DATA-MODEL §3). (6) **The Owner's decide dialog never pre-ticks "Count … as a day worked"** (nothing is automatic, decision 12; the checkpoint had it ticked). (7) **Bundle budget:** the note dialog (`add-note-button.tsx` → `extra-work-note-dialog.tsx`) and End day's overtime fields (`overtime-note-fields.tsx`) are `next/dynamic` imports: the Select they pull cost `/my-day` and `/today` 63 KB and put them over budget (832 → 768 KB). Keep new overlays on the strip lazy. (8) **e2e:** `e2e/working-day.spec.ts` (the gate's seeded people `gate-<kind>-<project>@`, reset per test), `e2e/extra-work.spec.ts` (serial, `extra-<project>@`, including the installed back orders of End day's select, the prompt's leave view, the Extra work tab, the comp leave select in the leave form and the Owner's Grant / Revoke); `signIn()` starts the day from the prompt by default (`{ day: "stop" }` to meet it). A modal (the End-day confirmation, the day sheet) hides the page from `getByRole`: read the strip by its text or close the layer first. (9) **Screens 3bA added or changed, for the phase-3b phone walk (375 and 430, installed, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** the Start-day prompt and its leave view; the strip on My Day / Today in every state (Not started, Started, ended, Yesterday not ended, On leave + I'm working today, Half day, Day off + I worked today); the End-day confirmation with the overtime note; Me → Sign out of this device; Attendance & leave → Extra work tab (balance, credits, notes, Add note dialog); the leave form's comp leave options; Approvals → Extra work (review sheet, decide dialog); `/people/[id]/leave` comp leave card (Grant, Revoke); the Owner's board with start and end times.
- **3cB mechanics (2026-09-28; read before touching a stand-in screen, the guide or the go-live runbook):** (1) **Stand-in copy is one file:** `src/app/(app)/_placeholder/stand-ins.ts`. When a phase builds a real screen (4.5 Tasks and the Admin's Approvals, 5.1 Alerts, 6.1 My Day, 6.2/6.3 Today, 6.4 Calendar, 6.5/9.x the Admin's Reports), it drops that entry, its `STAND_IN_SCREENS` row in `e2e/mobile.spec.ts` (which reads every stand-in per role at 375 and 430px: the copy, no build words, no sideways scroll, 44px targets, 130%/200% text) and, if nothing is left, `PlaceholderPage`. (2) **The stand-in skeletons trace the stand-ins** (`/review-phase 3c`, owner decision 2026-09-29; 3cB had left them tracing the future screens): `src/app/(app)/_placeholder/stand-in-skeleton.tsx` draws the `EmptyState` geometry (the dashed box, the 40px circle, one title line, the message lines measured in Chromium per stand-in at 375, 430 and from 464px, `STAND_IN_LINES`; rows past a width's count hide from that width up), and the seven stand-in routes' `loading.tsx` use it behind what the real screen already has (the attendance strip; the Owner's card and board on `/today`), picking the copy as the page does (`dayStandIn(role)`, `tasks.create`, `attendance.self`, the Admin branches of Approvals and Reports). **When a phase builds the real screen, restore the owner-specified shape** (`loading-routes.test.ts` names them: 4.5 Tasks cards and the Admin's Approvals list with two actions, 5.1 Alerts list, 6.1 My Day cards, 6.2 / 6.3 Today tiles, 6.4 Calendar's day strip, 6.5 / 9.3 the Admin's Reports tiles) and drop the stand-in's `STAND_IN_LINES` row with its copy. A copy change in `stand-ins.ts` re-measures the lines (a Playwright script against the built app, reading each message's height ÷ line-height). (3) **The smoke test takes two IST working days:** `attendance_start_day()` refuses on the joining day ("Your attendance starts tomorrow."), so day A is the join, leave and a claim, day B Start day → approve, End day with a claim, the month, deactivation. (4) **One-time links and link previews, fixed in the 3cB review fixes (must-fix 1; ROADMAP 5.2's prerequisite, done):** `/auth/confirm` used to verify the token on the **GET**, so anything fetching the link before the person (WhatsApp drawing a preview on the sender's phone while the message is typed, a mail scanner; Next answers HEAD with the GET handler too) spent it and the person saw "This link has expired or was already used". Now it is the **Continue to MaxOff** page (7). The runbook's "Disable link previews" advice is belt and braces, not a must. (5) **PDFs only from the laptop (owner instruction 2026-09-28):** never commit a PDF printed in a cloud session; Playwright's cached Chromium (`docs/guide/README.md`) is for previewing a layout only (it falls back from Segoe UI: the guide ran to 20 pages there, 18 with Edge). `build-pdf.ps1` prints both pages; the first-day PDF must be one page (the `@page` height, 720 mm, was sized with the container's wider fallback font, so Segoe UI leaves room). (6) **Screens 3cB changed, for the phase-3c phone walk (375 and 430, installed, both themes, 130% / 200% text):** the Owner's Today (the tile below the board), Tasks (Owner/Admin and Staff wording), Calendar, Alerts (the bell for Owner and Admin, the tab for Staff), an Admin's Today, Approvals and Reports, Staff My Day, and the Settings list's "Coming soon" rows (Owner and Admin; the row wraps and the badge drops under the label at large text since the review fixes: beside the badge the label kept 47px at 200% and broke mid-word, "Tas/k/typ/es"; `mobile.spec` measures every label ≥ 64px at 200% and no word breaks), **and the Continue page a one-time link lands on** (`/auth/confirm`, signed out, 375 and 430, both themes, 130% / 200%: the heading, one line, one solid button; back from it leaves). No new overlay or view control. (7) **The Continue page (3cB review fixes):** `src/app/(auth)/auth/confirm/page.tsx` replaces the `auth/confirm` route handler at the same URL, so the recovery template, the invite link and the bootstrap link keep working. **The GET verifies nothing:** it parses `token_hash` and `type` (`core/auth/link-params.ts`, pure, unit-tested; a missing or unknown value goes to `/login?reason=link` as before) and renders the login card's look with one **Continue to MaxOff** button (`core/auth/components/continue-form.tsx`) whose form carries the two values as hidden fields; HEAD renders the same (proved). Its response is `Cache-Control: no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` (`AUTH_LINK_ROUTE_HEADERS` in `core/http/response-headers.ts`, a `next.config.ts` rule after the global one) plus `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">`. **The POST verifies:** the server action `confirmAuthLink` (`core/auth/actions.ts`) calls the unchanged `verifyAuthLink()` (server-side `verifyOtp` with `token_hash`, ADR-0012, no PKCE) and redirects with `RedirectType.replace` to `/set-password`, or to `/login?reason=link` / `?reason=inactive` exactly as the GET handler did. Why: a one-time token spent by a fetch is a link the person can never open, and the go-live's own path (Copy invite link → WhatsApp) draws a preview. E2e: `e2e/auth-link.spec.ts` (desktop, 375, 430; its own seeded `link-<project>@maxoff.local` people, since GoTrue keeps one recovery token per person): a GET and a HEAD of a fresh link open no session and leave it usable, Continue verifies once, a second Continue shows the used copy, malformed links show it with nothing to tap, the headers and the meta tag, the mobile standard at 130/200%; `back-gesture.spec` proves back from the Continue page leaves the app installed at both widths; `e2e/helpers.ts` `followAuthLink()` opens every link the way a person does (the page, then Continue), used by `auth.spec`, `team.spec` and `back-gesture.spec`. `warm.setup.ts` warms the page with a well-formed, never-issued token (the page cannot tell).
- **3cA mechanics and owner steps (2026-09-28; read before 3cB):** (1) **Owner steps before 3c.3 can deploy** (none needs a session; never paste a secret into one): (a) the Cloudflare API token behind `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` needs **Zone → Workers Routes: Edit, DNS: Edit, SSL and Certificates: Edit on `maxoff.in`** for wrangler to create the Custom Domain, or attach `app.maxoff.in` once by hand (Workers & Pages → maxoff → Settings → Domains & Routes) and re-run the deploy; (b) delete `DAY_GATE_COOKIE_SECRET` from the `staging` and `production` environments (nothing reads it), and, when convenient, from the two Workers, where it stays as a Worker secret because `--secrets-file` is additive: `wrangler secret delete DAY_GATE_COOKIE_SECRET --env staging` and `… --env production`; (c) the **`backup` GitHub environment** (no reviewer) with secrets `SUPABASE_DB_PASSWORD`, `BACKUP_R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `BACKUP_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` and variables `SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF`, `SUPABASE_DB_POOLER_HOST` (Connect → Session pooler host), `R2_ACCOUNT_ID`, `BACKUP_R2_BUCKET=maxoff-backups-production`, `BACKUP_AGE_RECIPIENT` (the `age1…` public key from `age-keygen`; the private key offline), after creating the private bucket `maxoff-backups-production` with a 31-day lifecycle rule and an Object Read & Write token scoped to it (README → "Backups"); (d) the first `v*` tag on a green `main` commit after the 3c merge, approved in GitHub; the run's smoke step is the deployed check of the headers and the redirect; (e) the Owner bootstrap from README's PowerShell; (f) UptimeRobot on `https://app.maxoff.in/api/health`; (g) Actions → Backup → Run workflow, then the **production restore drill** (runbook) once, and every quarter. (2) **The contract migration reaches staging with this branch's Preview run** (it applies migrations to the shared staging database), so staging's `main` deploy (3b code) keeps working: the 3b app never called what was dropped (proved by grep before the migration was written: only `modules/attendance` read the three columns, and only for display). (3) **`/api/health` is the one database read outside a data/ layer** (ARCHITECTURE §3.1, `eslint.config.mjs`: `app → core/db/health.ts` by file); it needs `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` (always set on a deploy). (4) **The `logout` attendance event** is closed with a NOT VALID check rather than removed: staging carries rows with it and history is never destroyed; `domain/history.ts` still describes it ("Logged out") for those rows. (5) The three comp-leave refusals are the member routes; an **Owner correction to `comp_leave`** (`attendance_decide(correct)`) and `leave_owner_edit(type = comp_leave)` still create an approved comp leave request with no credit behind it (not in the review's list; under Ideas). (6) `scripts/smoke-deploy.sh` is curl-only and runnable by hand; a failed smoke fails the deploy run after the Worker is live (fix forward, or roll back in the dashboard). (7) The pooler: a GitHub runner has no IPv6 and Supabase's direct host needs it, so backups use the **session pooler** (`postgres.<ref>@<pooler host>:5432`); the dump's `auth` schema read by the `postgres` role is unproven on a hosted project until the production drill; the data-mode restore itself (`AUTH_MODE=data`, what a real recovery runs) is proven locally by `drill.sh data` against a container given GoTrue's own migrations, as `postgres`. (8) `restore.sh`'s cron-jobs check is a warning, not a failure: the jobs live in the platform's `cron` schema (never dumped), the restored migration history re-runs nothing, and a drill container has none; the runbook's step 3 re-creates the two with the migrations' exact SQL.
- **Contract migration after phase 3b merges — DONE in 3c.1 (`20260928131234_contract_phase3b`); the list stays for the record:** database: `attendance_touch()`, `session_logout()`, `app.attendance_logout()`, `attendance_today()` (replaced by `attendance_today_detail()`), `app.logout_not_recorded()` and its cron row `logout_not_recorded`, the columns `first_login_at` / `last_logout_at` / `logout_not_recorded` (**backfill `started_at` / `ended_at` from them first**, decision 31), the `logout` event action, and **comp leave without a credit** on every route (`/review-phase 3b`, each with a pgTAP refusal): `leave_submit('comp_leave')` (refuse it, or route it through the credit), `attendance_submit('comp_leave')` (it stays for the prompt's leave choice, which passes only leave / half day, so refuse `comp_leave` inside it) and `leave_request_change(…, type = comp_leave)` on a request with no credit. App and infrastructure: `DAY_GATE_COOKIE_SECRET` in `deploy.yml` (both environments) and in the `staging` / `production` GitHub environment secrets, the `maxoff_day` cookie left on devices (harmless). Already gone from the 3b app (the database side stays until then): the `(gate)` route, `requireDayGate` / `touchToday`, `IssueDayPass`, the `x-maxoff-path` header, `LogoutRow` and the account-menu / More-sheet log out.
- **CI runs less per push since 2026-09-27 (owner decision; ARCHITECTURE §18.1a):** a push to `main` or `phase-*` runs typecheck, lint, format, unit, build, budget and pgTAP; **Playwright runs only on pull requests, pushes to `main` (before Deploy) and a manual dispatch** ("Full tests", default on; always full on `main`). Docs-only pushes (`docs/**`, `*.md`, `screenshots/**`) run no CI and no preview; a docs-only pull request's jobs skip (the three required checks pass as skipped). **`/run-phase` dispatches the full run after each unit's last push and waits for it** (`gh workflow run CI --ref phase-<N> -f full=true`; `.claude/commands/run-phase.md`), and `/review-phase` does the same before its PR. CI concurrency is per branch **and** trigger, so a push never cancels a dispatched full run. This month: CI 125 runs / 1107 job-minutes, which the new rules would have made 82 / 409 plus one full run per unit; a code push on a phase branch drops from ≈ 12–14 min to ≈ 2–2.5 min.
- **Incident and rule (2026-09-27): a `main` deploy failed at `db push` because staging was ahead.** The first `main` hotfix after phase 3 started (PR #11, deploy run 36304315882) failed with "Remote migration versions not found in local migrations directory": the automatic `staging migrations` job (process change of the same morning) had applied the four 3A migrations to the shared staging database, and `main`'s deploy ran a blind `supabase db push` against it — a consequence of that change the orchestrator did not foresee. **Fix (PR #12):** `scripts/staging-migrations.sh`, used by `deploy.yml`'s staging job: lists first; nothing pending → skip with a notice naming what staging is ahead by; pending and not ahead → push; pending **and** ahead → **fail loudly** naming both sets (remedy: merge `main` into the phase branch and push, the preview job applies it, re-run the deploy); unreadable listing → push with a warning. Dry-run with `DRY_RUN_LIST=<listing file>`; all four paths were exercised before the PR. Production keeps the plain push. **Rule (owner decision, ARCHITECTURE §18, the `/run-phase` prompt): migrations on an open phase branch are expand-only** — add columns/tables/functions/policies, never rename, drop, narrow or change the meaning of anything `main` uses; removals in a follow-up migration after the merge. Flagged on ROADMAP **phase 3b** (the attendance and sessions rework, the case the owner named) and on 3.4 (unit 3B touches the person page). The `preview.yml` job still carries its own copy of the listing parse; folding it onto the script is a later item.
- **3.4 mechanics (read before touching `EditableRecord`, `DataTable`, `/people/[id]` or the client screens):** (1) **Routes:** `/clients/(list)` (route group, so its skeleton never wraps a client, the 2.9 rule); `/clients/[id]/(tabs)/` holds the layout (header + ⋯ + tabs; the state banner streams in under the tabs so it never moves them) and the Overview, `brand/`, `activity/`; `/clients/[id]/contacts/[contactId]` sits outside the group with its own header. `routeKey` has entries for `/people/[id](/leave|/attendance)` and `/clients/[id](/brand|/activity)`. Loaders `loadClient` / `loadPeople` (`clients/[id]/client.ts`) and `loadPerson` / `loadHistoryPerson` (`people/[id]/person.ts`) are `cache()`d per request. (2) **Links into a person moved:** the Today board and the pending-leave sheet open `/people/[id]/leave` (what `/people/[id]` used to be); People cards open the Profile. (3) **Edit from a menu:** `requestEdit(key)` (`core/ui/edit/edit-requests.ts`) starts the mounted record with that `editKey`, or waits up to 5 s for it to mount when the menu first switches views (`closeOverlaysThen(() => router.replace(profile))`); keys `member:<id>`, `client:<id>`, `contact:<id>`. `core/ui/edit/active-editor.ts` allows one record in edit mode per page. (4) **A URL write from inside an overlay must wait for it to back out:** `DataTable` filters are chosen inside an open select; writing `replaceState` there left the view on the select's (spent) entry and the next back dropped it. `changeView` writes through `closeOverlaysThen` and reads the newest view. Next's `history.replaceState` integration works (verified in 3.4: the URL keeps `?state=` through sheets and backs). **Since the phase 3 review the search text is never in the URL** (§18.2): it lives in `DataTable`'s state, and only a filter's id is written. (5) **A dialog closed by its button leaves its entry spent** (1.5 design): the next back press (and the on-screen back) lands on the same page first. Specs therefore reach the next screen by URL after a confirmed dialog, never by one back (e.g. `clients.spec` contacts). (6) **`updateClient` is a patch** (schema keys optional; the action writes only the keys sent; `customFields` validated only when sent), because the Overview has two client records. (7) ~~Brand colours and fonts typed one per line~~ **replaced in the 3B review** by the swatch and font lists (see "3B review decisions" (3)); `updateBrand` (arrays) is the one brand action. (8) **Close reason:** the Activity view shows it to `clients.manage` only, accepted as built by the owner in the 3B review (the database is unchanged; the API hardening is under Ideas / tech debt). (9) **`data-slot` passed to a primitive overwrites its own:** it removed the 16px/44px shell rules from `Input` and `SelectTrigger` (caught by `mobile.spec`); mark the wrapper or use another attribute (`data-filter`). (10) **e2e:** `e2e/clients.spec.ts` (desktop, mobile, mobile-lg; clients named per test and project, removed first), the `mobile.spec` "record screens" sweep (Profile, Leave, client views, contact at 100/130/200%), warm routes for the client screens (an unknown id still loads the code). (11) **Screens 3B added or changed, for the phase-3 phone walk:** `/clients` (search, filters, cards, ⋯ sheet, New client sheet), a client's Overview / Brand / Activity (header ⋯ menu, state banner, Details / notes edit mode, Add contact sheet, Assign/Change Admin and lifecycle confirmations, logo sheet), a contact page (⋯: Edit, Make primary, Archive with next primary, Restore), `/people/[id]` Profile (edit mode, header ⋯, the Owner's three tabs), People cards and sheet. **Added by the 3B review fixes:** the **Select sheet** on a phone (any select: e.g. the leave form's "Kind of leave" inside its dialog, the client list's State filter, a custom field's choice; title, ✓, tap, swipe-down, back order select sheet → dialog), and the **brand swatch editor** (a client's Brand: tap a swatch to copy; Edit: colour picker, hex field, add / move / remove colour and font rows, the named-change confirmation) at 375 and 430 px, both themes, 130% and 200% text.
- **Phase 3 review decisions (2026-09-27, owner; `/review-phase 3`, Opus 5.5):** must fix: (1) **the client close reason is Owner-only in the database** (`f3c5035`, migration `20260927143324`, table `client_close_reasons`, pgTAP `20`; the deactivation reason re-checked: still Owner-only); (2) **members never create or complete a file row themselves** (`f7a718b`, migration `20260927144619`: no API insert on `files`; `file_begin()` builds the key and `file_complete(id, uploader, …)` is service_role only, called by the storage actions after their checks, the one use of the service client inside a member's request, ARCHITECTURE §11; the adapter refuses dot segments; pgTAP `18` rewritten); (3) **a demoted Admin loses their clients, and no client is ever left without an Admin** (`8e6d114`, migration `20260927145614`: `clients.edit_assigned` on the Admin branch of `client_visible` / `admin_client_ids` / two policies; members trigger `client_admin_guard` refuses a demotion or deactivation while they run a client; `client_hand_over(from_admin, moves)`; the Owner's Profile Save confirmation and Deactivate dialog offer "Move Ravi's N clients to [Admin ▾]", one for all or per client (`ClientHandover`, `EditableRecord.confirmation`, `ConfirmDialog.confirmDisabled`); pgTAP `21`, `e2e/handover.spec.ts`, seed people `handover-<project>@`). Should fix (owner: all now): (4) **the cleanup never deletes a file a record still uses** (`f15ce24`, migration `20260927151606`: one upload, one place; an archived file is never attached again; the archived branch spares referenced files; pgTAP `22`); (5) **search text never enters the URL** (`5c46c3b`: `DataTable` keeps it in state, filter ids stay in `?<id>=`, ARCHITECTURE §14.1 corrected to §18.2); (6) the missing installed-mode back-gesture specs (see the session log); (7) **the database checks custom-field values and brand lists** (`ec5e411`, migration `20260927152618`: ≤ 32 KB, every changed key needs an active definition in scope and a value of its type, url https only in zod too; brand colours ≤ 12, fonts ≤ 6 of the app's shape; pgTAP `23`). **Number fields stay allowed on clients and contacts**; the field form shows "Admins can see this field. Amounts belong in project billing (Owner only)." for a number field or any client/contact field (`e2eaadd`, PRODUCT §4.16). **Phone walk** (owner, Samsung S23, gesture navigation): every screen passed but one: (8) **the sticky save bar covered the last field** in edit mode (a person's Profile: "Job title"); the reserve rule sat in `@layer base`, where the shell's padding utility always won, so it had never applied. Fixed in `StickyActions` + `globals.css` (`e92d9ba`, measured height `--app-sticky-actions-h`, utilities-layer reserve, `scroll-margin-bottom` on controls; ARCHITECTURE §14.1), `e2e/sticky-actions.spec.ts`. The review's "deploy.yml does not map the S3/CRON secrets" finding was stale (mapped since PR #11; the staging environment holds all five).
- **3B review decisions (2026-09-27, owner; built the same day, recorded in ARCHITECTURE §14.1/§14.2, PRODUCT §4.4, PERMISSIONS §1 screens, DATA-MODEL §9, WORKFLOWS §4a/§8):** (1) **The Select primitive on phones** (`core/ui/primitives/select.tsx`, every select in the app: 11 callers, none changed): below 768px a tap opens a **nested bottom sheet** (`data-slot="select-sheet"`, a `Sheet`, so its own overlay layer inside a sheet or a dialog) titled with the field's label (`labelOf`: `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `<label for>`), 48px rows (`role="option"` buttons in a `role="listbox"`), a ✓ on the current value, a tap picks and closes, swipe-down / back / ✕ / backdrop close without a change; the sheet closes with no exit animation (the choice lands at once and the dialog beneath is the only one left). From 768px a **popper below the trigger at the trigger's width**, never item-aligned (`position` is no longer a prop). The Radix root stays mounted on every width (always controlled, `""` = nothing chosen), so the trigger text, keyboard typeahead and the hidden form input are unchanged; the phone/desktop choice is read at the moment of opening (`PHONE_QUERY`), never at render. `SelectGroup` / `SelectLabel` / `SelectSeparator` were unused and are gone (they cannot render in the sheet). **Swipe-down** is `core/ui/overlay/swipe-dismiss.ts` (pointer drag, 80 px or a flick of at least 0.5 px/ms over 24 px, downward only, only with the list at its top; a drag never becomes a tap), used by the select sheet only for now. `e2e/select.spec.ts` (375, 430: title, 48px rows, ✓, tap, swipe; desktop: popper geometry) and `back-gesture.spec.ts` (installed: back closes the select sheet, then the dialog, then the page). (2) **An Admin's client list opens on Active** too (`client-list.tsx` `defaultValue: "active"`). (3) **Brand colours are a swatch list, fonts a simple list:** read mode shows a chip, the name and the hex per colour (a row is a button: a tap copies the hex, toast "Copied #E11D48"), and each font's name with its optional note; edit mode is rows (colour: the native colour picker, a name and a hex field that types as `#` and six upper-case digits; font: a name and a note), with Add, Move up / down and Remove (neutral `ghost` icon buttons: a removal is only a draft until the named Save). Built as `EditableRecord`'s `extra` (new `first` and `owns` options) with `updateBrand` (arrays); each change is a named line (`domain/brand-changes.ts`: added, removed, "will change to", "will be put in a new order"). **No migration:** `client_brand.colors` / `fonts` were already structured jsonb (`[{name, hex}]`, `[{family, usage}]`); only the "one per line" editing (`brand-lines.ts`, `updateBrandText`) is gone. (4) **Accepted as built, no change:** the close reason is Owner-only (the Activity view shows it to `clients.manage` only; PRODUCT §4.4, PERMISSIONS screens); Leave and Attendance stay separate tabs on `/people/[id]`; a client is activated only after an Admin is assigned; a client's tabs are Overview / Brand / Activity; the Owner's own details are edited on /me only. (5) **Orphaned `ready` files are cleaned after 7 days:** migration `20260927130730_files_orphan_cleanup.sql` re-creates `file_cleanup_candidates(archived_before, pending_before, orphaned_before, batch)` (the 3-argument form dropped: files are phase-3 only, nothing on `main` calls it): a `ready`, unarchived original older than the window that **no single-column foreign key to `files` references** (read from the catalog on each run, so phase 5's submissions are protected by declaring their FK) is a candidate, and a preview follows its original (a candidate once the original is one or is deleted). `ORPHANED_RETENTION_DAYS = 7` (`core/storage/limits.ts`). **Review fixes (migration `20260927134340_files_cleanup_keep_referenced.sql`):** a preview is a candidate only when no foreign key references the preview itself (ADR-0010: a submission's preview outlives its deleted local original), and `files_reference_guard` refuses to attach an upload 6 days old or more ("This upload has expired. Upload the file again."), so a sheet left open for a week can never attach a file the cleanup is deleting. pgTAP `19` (18) covers each path; `18` moved to the new signature. **Also from the review:** a tap on the already-chosen row closes the select sheet without calling `onValueChange`; the sheet is `touch-none` (the list `touch-pan-y` only when it scrolls) so a real touch swipe is not taken by the browser, proved by a DevTools-protocol touch drag in `select.spec.ts` (from the title, and from a row, which it never picks).
- **Kickoff 3 decisions (2026-09-27, owner; recorded in PRODUCT §4.4/§4.16, PERMISSIONS §1 ²/§3, WORKFLOWS §4/§4a/§9, DATA-MODEL §2/§4/§9, ARCHITECTURE §11, ROADMAP 3.2–3.4):** (1) client name unique, case-insensitive, among clients not Inactive. (2) Inactive is the end state; no archive action (`archived_at` reserved). (3) Admin assignment notifies the new Admin and the previous one if active (named in the function comment, delivered by 5.1). (4) exactly one primary contact once any contact exists; archiving the primary asks for the next; no contact uniqueness. (5) GSTIN 15-char format blocking when given; website and Drive link `https://` only.
